# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-10-27)

## Corpus Check
- 4766 files · ~10,879,531 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 110 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 34, .css 16, .entitlements 11)

## Summary
- 65883 nodes · 190149 edges · 1377 communities (1252 shown, 125 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 7848 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `6b672f9e`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
